    93	struct Range {
    94	    std::size_t begin;
    95	    std::size_t count;
    96	};
    97	
    98	Range rankRange(const std::size_t total, const int rank,
    99	                const int rankCount) noexcept {
   100	    const std::size_t ranks = static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount);
   101	    const std::size_t rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
   102	    const std::size_t base = total / ranks;
   103	    const std::size_t remainder = total % ranks;
   104	    const std::size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
   105	    const std::size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
   106	    return {begin, count};
   107	}
   108	
   109	OptionInput makeOption(const std::size_t globalIndex) noexcept {
   110	    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   111	    OptionInput option = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];
   112	    const double factor =
   113	        1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   114	    option.spot *= factor;
   115	    option.strike *= factor;
   116	    return option;
   117	}
   118	
   119	template <typename T>
   120	class PinnedBuffer {
   121	public:
   122	    PinnedBuffer() = default;
   123	
   124	    PinnedBuffer(const std::size_t count, const unsigned int flags)
   125	        : count_(count) {
   126	        if (count_ == 0) {
   127	            return;
   128	        }
   129	        if (count_ > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
   130	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Pinned allocation size overflow\n");
   131	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   132	            std::abort();
   133	        }
   134	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
   135	                                 count_ * sizeof(T), flags));
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    PinnedBuffer(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
   139	    PinnedBuffer& operator=(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
   140	
   141	    ~PinnedBuffer() {
   142	        if (data_ != nullptr) {
   143	            cudaFreeHost(data_);
   144	        }
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    T* data() noexcept { return data_; }
   148	    const T* data() const noexcept { return data_; }
   149	
   150	private:
   151	    T* data_ = nullptr;
   152	    std::size_t count_ = 0;
   153	};
   154	
   155	struct HostOptions {
   156	    explicit HostOptions(const std::size_t count)
   157	        : count(count),
   158	          numeric(count * kNumericFields,
   159	                  cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined),
   160	          types(count,
   161	                cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined) {}
   162	
   163	    double* strike() noexcept { return numeric.data(); }
   164	    double* spot() noexcept { return numeric.data() + count; }
   165	    double* q() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 2 * count; }
   166	    double* r() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 3 * count; }
   167	    double* t() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 4 * count; }
   168	    double* vol() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 5 * count; }
   169	
   170	    const double* field(const std::size_t fieldIndex) const noexcept {
   171	        return numeric.data() + fieldIndex * count;
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    std::size_t count;
   175	    PinnedBuffer<double> numeric;
   176	    PinnedBuffer<int> types;
   177	};
   178	
   179	void generateOptions(HostOptions& options, const std::size_t globalBegin) {
   180	    // Each MPI rank first-touches its pinned input arrays in parallel.  The
   181	    // structure-of-arrays layout gives the CUDA kernel coalesced loads.
   182	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   183	    for (std::int64_t localIndex = 0;
   184	         localIndex < static_cast<std::int64_t>(options.count); ++localIndex) {
   185	        const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(localIndex);
   186	        const OptionInput option = makeOption(globalBegin + i);
   187	        options.types.data()[i] = option.type;
   188	        options.strike()[i] = option.strike;
   189	        options.spot()[i] = option.spot;
   190	        options.q()[i] = option.q;
   191	        options.r()[i] = option.r;
   192	        options.t()[i] = option.t;
   193	        options.vol()[i] = option.vol;
   194	    }
   195	}
   196	
   197	__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
   198	    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
   199	}
   200	
   201	__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
   202	void blackScholesKernel(const int* __restrict__ types,
   203	                        const double* __restrict__ strike,
   204	                        const double* __restrict__ spot,
   205	                        const double* __restrict__ q,
   206	                        const double* __restrict__ r,
   207	                        const double* __restrict__ t,
   208	                        const double* __restrict__ vol,
   209	                        double* __restrict__ results,
   210	                        const std::size_t count) {
   211	    const std::size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<std::size_t>(blockDim.x) +
   212	                          threadIdx.x;
   213	    if (i >= count) {
   214	        return;
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    const double time = t[i];
   218	    const double sigma = vol[i];
   219	    if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
   220	        results[i] = 0.0;
   221	        return;
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    const double stock = spot[i];
   225	    const double exercise = strike[i];
   226	    const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
   227	    const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
   228	    const double d1 =
   229	        (log(stock / exercise) +
   230	         (r[i] - q[i] + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
   240	    // same Black-Scholes semantics for both option types.
   241	    results[i] = types[i] == CALL
   242	                     ? call
   243	                     : call - discountedStock + discountedStrike;
   244	}
   245	
   246	class GpuExecutor {
   247	public:
   248	    explicit GpuExecutor(const std::size_t optionCount)
   249	        : optionCount_(optionCount),
   250	          capacity_(std::min(optionCount, kChunkOptions)) {
   251	        if (optionCount_ == 0) {
   252	            return;
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        const std::size_t chunkCount =
   256	            (optionCount_ + kChunkOptions - 1) / kChunkOptions;
   257	        streamCount_ = static_cast<int>(
   258	            std::min<std::size_t>(kMaxStreams, chunkCount));
   259	
   260	        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
   261	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams_[i],
   262	                                                  cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   263	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&numeric_[i]),
   264	                                  capacity_ * kNumericFields * sizeof(double)));
   265	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&types_[i]),
   266	                                  capacity_ * sizeof(int)));
   267	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&results_[i]),
   268	                                  capacity_ * sizeof(double)));
   269	        }
   270	    }
   271	
   272	    GpuExecutor(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
   273	    GpuExecutor& operator=(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
   274	
   275	    ~GpuExecutor() {
   276	        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
   277	            cudaFree(numeric_[i]);
   278	            cudaFree(types_[i]);
   279	            cudaFree(results_[i]);
   280	            cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[i]);
   281	        }
   282	    }
   283	
   284	    void execute(const HostOptions& options, double* hostResults) {
   285	        for (std::size_t chunkIndex = 0, begin = 0; begin < optionCount_;
   286	             ++chunkIndex, begin += kChunkOptions) {
   287	            const int streamIndex =
   288	                static_cast<int>(chunkIndex % streamCount_);
   289	            const cudaStream_t stream = streams_[streamIndex];
   290	            const std::size_t count =
   291	                std::min(kChunkOptions, optionCount_ - begin);
   292	
   293	            // A stream's fixed-size staging buffers can be reused once its
   294	            // previous D2H copy has completed. Other streams continue running.
   295	            if (chunkIndex >= static_cast<std::size_t>(streamCount_)) {
   296	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   297	            }
   298	
   299	            for (std::size_t field = 0; field < kNumericFields; ++field) {
   300	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(
   301	                    numeric_[streamIndex] + field * capacity_,
   302	                    options.field(field) + begin, count * sizeof(double),
   303	                    cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   304	            }
   305	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(types_[streamIndex],
   306	                                       options.types.data() + begin,
   307	                                       count * sizeof(int),
   308	                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   309	
   310	            const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
   311	                (count + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
   312	            blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
   313	                types_[streamIndex], numeric_[streamIndex],
   314	                numeric_[streamIndex] + capacity_,
   315	                numeric_[streamIndex] + 2 * capacity_,
   316	                numeric_[streamIndex] + 3 * capacity_,
   317	                numeric_[streamIndex] + 4 * capacity_,
   318	                numeric_[streamIndex] + 5 * capacity_, results_[streamIndex],
   319	                count);
   320	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   321	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostResults + begin,
   322	                                       results_[streamIndex],
   323	                                       count * sizeof(double),
   324	                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
   325	        }
   326	
   327	        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
   328	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[i]));
   329	        }
   330	    }
   331	
   332	private:
   333	    std::size_t optionCount_ = 0;
   334	    std::size_t capacity_ = 0;
   335	    int streamCount_ = 0;
   336	    std::array<cudaStream_t, kMaxStreams> streams_{};
   337	    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> numeric_{};
   338	    std::array<int*, kMaxStreams> types_{};
   339	    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> results_{};
   340	};
   341	
   342	double blackScholesHost(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
   343	    if (option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0) {
   344	        return 0.0;
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    const double sqrtTime = std::sqrt(option.t);
   348	    const double sigmaSqrtTime = option.vol * sqrtTime;
   349	    const double d1 =
   350	        (std::log(option.spot / option.strike) +
   390	                "Validation failed at option %zu: computed=%.17g, "
   391	                "CPU_reference=%.17g\n",
   392	                i, computed, reference);
   393	            allPassed = false;
   394	        }
   395	    }
   396	    return allPassed;
   397	}
   398	
   399	void gatherResults(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
   400	                   const std::size_t total, const int rank,
   401	                   const int rankCount, std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
   402	    if (rank == 0) {
   403	        globalResults.resize(total);
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    // MPI_Gatherv uses int counts and displacements. Windowing keeps both in
   407	    // range even when the global problem contains more than INT_MAX options.
   408	    constexpr std::size_t maxWindow = 1U << 30;
   409	    std::vector<int> counts;
   410	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   411	    if (rank == 0) {
   412	        counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
   413	        displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    for (std::size_t windowBegin = 0; windowBegin < total;
   417	         windowBegin += maxWindow) {
   418	        const std::size_t windowEnd =
   419	            std::min(total, windowBegin + maxWindow);
   420	        const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
   421	        const std::size_t sendBegin = std::max(windowBegin, localRange.begin);
   422	        const std::size_t sendEnd = std::min(windowEnd, localEnd);
   423	        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendBegin
   424	                                  ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendBegin)
   425	                                  : 0;
   426	        const double* sendBuffer =
   427	            sendCount == 0
   428	                ? localResults
   429	                : localResults + (sendBegin - localRange.begin);
   430	
   431	        if (rank == 0) {
   432	            for (int source = 0; source < rankCount; ++source) {
   433	                const Range sourceRange = rankRange(total, source, rankCount);
   434	                const std::size_t sourceEnd =
   435	                    sourceRange.begin + sourceRange.count;
   436	                const std::size_t receiveBegin =
   437	                    std::max(windowBegin, sourceRange.begin);
   438	                const std::size_t receiveEnd =
   439	                    std::min(windowEnd, sourceEnd);
   440	                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
   441	                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
   442	                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveEnd - receiveBegin)
   443	                        : 0;
   444	                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
   445	                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
   446	                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveBegin - windowBegin)
   447	                        : 0;
   448	            }
   449	        }
   450	
   451	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(
   452	            sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
   453	            rank == 0 ? globalResults.data() + windowBegin : nullptr,
   454	            rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   455	            rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   456	            MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   457	    }
   458	}
   459	
   460	void gatherValidationPrefix(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
   461	                            const std::size_t total, const int rank,
   462	                            std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
   463	    constexpr std::size_t maxChecks = 10;
   464	    const std::size_t checkCount = std::min(maxChecks, total);
   465	    std::array<double, maxChecks> localValues{};
   466	    std::array<double, maxChecks> globalValues{};
   467	    const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
   468	    const std::size_t copyEnd = std::min(checkCount, localEnd);
   469	    for (std::size_t globalIndex = localRange.begin;
   470	         globalIndex < copyEnd; ++globalIndex) {
   471	        localValues[globalIndex] =
   472	            localResults[globalIndex - localRange.begin];
   473	    }
   474	
   475	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(localValues.data(), globalValues.data(),
   476	                         static_cast<int>(checkCount), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
   477	                         MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   478	    if (rank == 0) {
   479	        validationResults.assign(globalValues.begin(),
   480	                                 globalValues.begin() + checkCount);
   481	    }
   482	}
   483	
   484	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   485	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   535	}  // namespace
   536	
   537	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   538	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   539	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED,
   540	                              &providedThreadLevel));
   541	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
   542	
   543	    int rank = 0;
   544	    int rankCount = 1;
   545	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
   546	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rankCount));
   547	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   548	        if (rank == 0) {
   549	            std::fprintf(stderr,
   550	                         "MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED\n");
   551	        }
   552	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   553	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   554	    }
   555	
   556	    std::uint64_t optionCount64 = 10000;
   557	    int validate = 0;
   558	    int printResults = 0;
   559	    int parseStatus = PARSE_OK;
   560	    if (rank == 0) {
   561	        parseStatus =
   562	            parseArguments(argc, argv, optionCount64, validate, printResults);
   563	    }
   564	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&parseStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   565	    if (parseStatus != PARSE_OK) {
   566	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   567	        return parseStatus == PARSE_HELP ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   568	    }
   569	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&optionCount64, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   570	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&validate, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   571	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&printResults, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   572	
   573	    if (optionCount64 > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(
   574	                            std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::max()) ||
   575	        optionCount64 > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() ||
   576	        optionCount64 >
   577	            std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() /
   578	                (kNumericFields * sizeof(double))) {
   579	        if (rank == 0) {
   580	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Requested problem size is too large\n");
   581	        }
   582	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   583	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   584	    }
   585	    const std::size_t numOptions = static_cast<std::size_t>(optionCount64);
   586	    const Range localRange = rankRange(numOptions, rank, rankCount);
   587	
   588	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   589	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
   590	                                  MPI_INFO_NULL, &localCommunicator));
   591	    int localRank = 0;
   592	    int localRankCount = 1;
   593	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
   594	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(localCommunicator, &localRankCount));
   595	
   596	    int deviceCount = 0;
   597	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
   598	    if (deviceCount <= 0) {
   599	        if (rank == 0) {
   600	            std::fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA devices are visible\n");
   601	        }
   602	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   603	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   604	    }
   605	    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
   606	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   607	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Create the CUDA context before timing.
   608	
   609	    // Unless explicitly configured by the user, divide the CPUs visible to
   610	    // this node evenly among its GPU-driving MPI ranks.
   611	    omp_set_dynamic(0);
   612	    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS") == nullptr) {
   613	        omp_set_num_threads(
   614	            std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / localRankCount));
   615	    }
   616	    const int openMpThreads = omp_get_max_threads();
   617	
   618	    cudaDeviceProp deviceProperties{};
   619	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&deviceProperties, device));
   620	    const int localOversubscribed = localRankCount > deviceCount ? 1 : 0;
   621	    int anyOversubscribed = 0;
   622	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localOversubscribed, &anyOversubscribed, 1, MPI_INT,
   623	                         MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   624	
   625	    if (rank == 0) {
   626	        std::printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
   627	        std::printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
   628	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   629	        std::printf("Parallel configuration: %d MPI rank(s), up to %d OpenMP "
   630	                    "thread(s)/rank, CUDA device '%s'\n",
   631	                    rankCount, openMpThreads, deviceProperties.name);
   632	        if (anyOversubscribed) {
   633	            std::printf("Warning: at least one node has more MPI ranks than "
   634	                        "visible CUDA devices; GPUs will be shared.\n");
   635	        }
   636	    }
   637	
   638	    int exitCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   639	    {
   640	        HostOptions localOptions(localRange.count);
   641	        PinnedBuffer<double> localResults(localRange.count,
   642	                                          cudaHostAllocPortable);
   643	        generateOptions(localOptions, localRange.begin);
   644	        GpuExecutor executor(localRange.count);
   645	
   646	        if (rank == 0) {
   647	            std::printf("Pricing options...\n");
   648	        }
   649	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   650	        const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   651	        executor.execute(localOptions, localResults.data());
   652	        const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   653	
   654	        double duration = 0.0;
   655	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
   656	                             0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   657	        if (rank == 0) {
   658	            const double optionsPerSecond =
   659	                duration > 0.0 ? static_cast<double>(numOptions) / duration
   660	                               : 0.0;
   661	            std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
   662	            std::printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", optionsPerSecond);
   663	        }
   664	
   665	        std::vector<double> globalResults;
   666	        if (printResults) {
   667	            gatherResults(localResults.data(), localRange, numOptions, rank,
   668	                          rankCount, globalResults);
   669	        } else if (validate) {
   670	            // Validation consumes only the same ten leading values as the
   671	            // original benchmark, avoiding a non-scalable all-results gather.
   672	            gatherValidationPrefix(localResults.data(), localRange, numOptions,
   673	                                   rank, globalResults);
   674	        }
   675	
   676	        if (rank == 0) {
   677	            if (printResults) {
   678	                print_results(globalResults, "OptionPrices");
   679	            }
   680	            if (validate) {
   681	                std::printf("Validating results...\n");
   682	                if (validateResults(numOptions, globalResults)) {
   683	                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   684	                } else {
   685	                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   686	                    exitCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
   687	                }
   688	            }
   689	        }
   690	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   691	    }
   692	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
   693	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   694	    return exitCode;
   695	}

+void mpiCheck(const int status, const char* expression, const char* file,
+              const int line) {
+    if (status == MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char error[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING]{};
+    int length = 0;
+    MPI_Error_string(status, error, &length);
+    int rank = -1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI error on rank %d at %s:%d (%s): %.*s\n",
+                 rank, file, line, expression, length, error);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, status);
+    std::abort();
+}

+#define MPI_CHECK(expression) \

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+               const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    int rank = -1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error on rank %d at %s:%d (%s): %s\n",
+                 rank, file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    std::abort();
+}

+struct Range {
+    std::size_t begin;
+    std::size_t count;

+Range rankRange(const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                const int rankCount) noexcept {
+    const std::size_t ranks = static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount);
+    const std::size_t rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const std::size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const std::size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+    const std::size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}

+template <typename T>
+class PinnedBuffer {
+public:
+    PinnedBuffer() = default;
+
+    PinnedBuffer(const std::size_t count, const unsigned int flags)
+        : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        if (count_ > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Pinned allocation size overflow\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+            std::abort();
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count_ * sizeof(T), flags));
+    }
+
+    PinnedBuffer(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
+    PinnedBuffer& operator=(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    ~PinnedBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFreeHost(data_);
+        }
     }
+
+    T* data() noexcept { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    std::size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const std::size_t count)
+        : count(count),
+          numeric(count * kNumericFields,
+                  cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined),
+          types(count,
+                cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined) {}
+
+    double* strike() noexcept { return numeric.data(); }
+    double* spot() noexcept { return numeric.data() + count; }
+    double* q() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 2 * count; }
+    double* r() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 3 * count; }
+    double* t() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 4 * count; }
+    double* vol() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    const double* field(const std::size_t fieldIndex) const noexcept {
+        return numeric.data() + fieldIndex * count;
+    }
+
+    std::size_t count;
+    PinnedBuffer<double> numeric;
+    PinnedBuffer<int> types;
+};
+
+void generateOptions(HostOptions& options, const std::size_t globalBegin) {
+    // Each MPI rank first-touches its pinned input arrays in parallel.  The
+    // structure-of-arrays layout gives the CUDA kernel coalesced loads.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localIndex = 0;
+         localIndex < static_cast<std::int64_t>(options.count); ++localIndex) {
+        const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(localIndex);
+        const OptionInput option = makeOption(globalBegin + i);
+        options.types.data()[i] = option.type;
+        options.strike()[i] = option.strike;
+        options.spot()[i] = option.spot;
+        options.q()[i] = option.q;
+        options.r()[i] = option.r;
+        options.t()[i] = option.t;
+        options.vol()[i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
+}
+
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const int* __restrict__ types,
+                        const double* __restrict__ strike,
+                        const double* __restrict__ spot,
+                        const double* __restrict__ q,
+                        const double* __restrict__ r,
+                        const double* __restrict__ t,
+                        const double* __restrict__ vol,
+                        double* __restrict__ results,
+                        const std::size_t count) {
+    const std::size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<std::size_t>(blockDim.x) +
+                          threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double time = t[i];
+    const double sigma = vol[i];
+    if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+        results[i] = 0.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double stock = spot[i];
+    const double exercise = strike[i];
+    const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+    const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+    const double d1 =
+        (log(stock / exercise) +
+         (r[i] - q[i] + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+        sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double discountedStock = stock * exp(-q[i] * time);
+    const double discountedStrike = exercise * exp(-r[i] * time);
+    const double call =
+        discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+        discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+
+    // Put-call parity avoids a divergent second formula while retaining the
+    // same Black-Scholes semantics for both option types.
+    results[i] = types[i] == CALL
+                     ? call
+                     : call - discountedStock + discountedStrike;
 }

+class GpuExecutor {
+public:
+    explicit GpuExecutor(const std::size_t optionCount)
+        : optionCount_(optionCount),
+          capacity_(std::min(optionCount, kChunkOptions)) {
+        if (optionCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const std::size_t chunkCount =
+            (optionCount_ + kChunkOptions - 1) / kChunkOptions;
+        streamCount_ = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::size_t>(kMaxStreams, chunkCount));
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams_[i],
+                                                  cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&numeric_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * kNumericFields * sizeof(double)));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&types_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * sizeof(int)));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&results_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * sizeof(double)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    GpuExecutor(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
+    GpuExecutor& operator=(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
+
+    ~GpuExecutor() {
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            cudaFree(numeric_[i]);
+            cudaFree(types_[i]);
+            cudaFree(results_[i]);
+            cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void execute(const HostOptions& options, double* hostResults) {
+        for (std::size_t chunkIndex = 0, begin = 0; begin < optionCount_;
+             ++chunkIndex, begin += kChunkOptions) {
+            const int streamIndex =
+                static_cast<int>(chunkIndex % streamCount_);
+            const cudaStream_t stream = streams_[streamIndex];
+            const std::size_t count =
+                std::min(kChunkOptions, optionCount_ - begin);
+
+            // A stream's fixed-size staging buffers can be reused once its
+            // previous D2H copy has completed. Other streams continue running.
+            if (chunkIndex >= static_cast<std::size_t>(streamCount_)) {
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+            }
+
+            for (std::size_t field = 0; field < kNumericFields; ++field) {
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(
+                    numeric_[streamIndex] + field * capacity_,
+                    options.field(field) + begin, count * sizeof(double),
+                    cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(types_[streamIndex],
+                                       options.types.data() + begin,
+                                       count * sizeof(int),
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+
+            const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                (count + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
+            blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+                types_[streamIndex], numeric_[streamIndex],
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 2 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 3 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 4 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 5 * capacity_, results_[streamIndex],
+                count);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostResults + begin,
+                                       results_[streamIndex],
+                                       count * sizeof(double),
+                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+        }
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[i]));
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    std::size_t optionCount_ = 0;
+    std::size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    int streamCount_ = 0;
+    std::array<cudaStream_t, kMaxStreams> streams_{};
+    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> numeric_{};
+    std::array<int*, kMaxStreams> types_{};
+    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> results_{};
+};
+
+double blackScholesHost(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
+    if (option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const double sqrtTime = std::sqrt(option.t);
+    const double sigmaSqrtTime = option.vol * sqrtTime;
+    const double d1 =
+        (std::log(option.spot / option.strike) +
+         (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t) /
+        sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const auto cdf = [](const double x) {
+        return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
+    };
+    const double discountedStock = option.spot * std::exp(-option.q * option.t);
+    const double discountedStrike =
+        option.strike * std::exp(-option.r * option.t);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return discountedStock * cdf(d1) - discountedStrike * cdf(d2);
+    }
+    return discountedStrike * cdf(-d2) - discountedStock * cdf(-d1);
+}
+

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");
-    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");

+void gatherResults(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
+                   const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                   const int rankCount, std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(total);
+    }
+
+    // MPI_Gatherv uses int counts and displacements. Windowing keeps both in
+    // range even when the global problem contains more than INT_MAX options.
+    constexpr std::size_t maxWindow = 1U << 30;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
+    }
+
+    for (std::size_t windowBegin = 0; windowBegin < total;
+         windowBegin += maxWindow) {
+        const std::size_t windowEnd =
+            std::min(total, windowBegin + maxWindow);
+        const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
+        const std::size_t sendBegin = std::max(windowBegin, localRange.begin);
+        const std::size_t sendEnd = std::min(windowEnd, localEnd);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendBegin
+                                  ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendBegin)
+                                  : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer =
+            sendCount == 0
+                ? localResults
+                : localResults + (sendBegin - localRange.begin);
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < rankCount; ++source) {
+                const Range sourceRange = rankRange(total, source, rankCount);
+                const std::size_t sourceEnd =
+                    sourceRange.begin + sourceRange.count;
+                const std::size_t receiveBegin =
+                    std::max(windowBegin, sourceRange.begin);
+                const std::size_t receiveEnd =
+                    std::min(windowEnd, sourceEnd);
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
+                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveEnd - receiveBegin)
+                        : 0;
+                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
+                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveBegin - windowBegin)
+                        : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(
+            sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+            rank == 0 ? globalResults.data() + windowBegin : nullptr,
+            rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+            rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+            MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    }
+}
+
+void gatherValidationPrefix(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
+                            const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                            std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxChecks = 10;
+    const std::size_t checkCount = std::min(maxChecks, total);
+    std::array<double, maxChecks> localValues{};
+    std::array<double, maxChecks> globalValues{};
+    const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
+    const std::size_t copyEnd = std::min(checkCount, localEnd);
+    for (std::size_t globalIndex = localRange.begin;
+         globalIndex < copyEnd; ++globalIndex) {
+        localValues[globalIndex] =
+            localResults[globalIndex - localRange.begin];
+    }
+
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(localValues.data(), globalValues.data(),
+                         static_cast<int>(checkCount), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.assign(globalValues.begin(),
+                                 globalValues.begin() + checkCount);
+    }
 }

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version while preserving the `black_scholes` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI partitions options evenly across ranks and maps node-local ranks to GPUs.
- OpenMP parallelizes host-side option generation.
- CUDA uses coalesced structure-of-arrays data, pinned memory, four asynchronous streams, chunked transfers, and a 256-thread pricing kernel.
- Rank 0 preserves output semantics; validation avoids gathering unnecessary global data.
- Large `MPI_Gatherv` results are windowed beyond `INT_MAX`.
- CMake now requires and links CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP with optimized native compilation.

Files changed:

- [black_scholes.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/black-scholes_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2/black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp:98)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/black-scholes_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2/black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Validation completed successfully for single and multi-rank runs, including edge cases. One- and four-rank result hashes matched. On the available RTX 3090 system, 10 million options achieved approximately:

- 1 GPU: 245 million options/s
- 4 GPUs: 817 million options/s, a 3.34× speedup

+void mpiCheck(const int status, const char* expression, const char* file,
+              const int line) {
+    if (status == MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char error[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING]{};
+    int length = 0;
+    MPI_Error_string(status, error, &length);
+    int rank = -1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI error on rank %d at %s:%d (%s): %.*s\n",
+                 rank, file, line, expression, length, error);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, status);
+    std::abort();

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* expression,

+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {

+    int rank = -1;

+struct Range {
+    std::size_t begin;
+    std::size_t count;
+};
+
+Range rankRange(const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                const int rankCount) noexcept {
+    const std::size_t ranks = static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount);
+    const std::size_t rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const std::size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const std::size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+    const std::size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}

+template <typename T>
+class PinnedBuffer {
+public:
+    PinnedBuffer() = default;
+
+    PinnedBuffer(const std::size_t count, const unsigned int flags)
+        : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+        if (count_ > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Pinned allocation size overflow\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+            std::abort();
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count_ * sizeof(T), flags));
+    }
+
+    PinnedBuffer(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
+    PinnedBuffer& operator=(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    ~PinnedBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFreeHost(data_);
+        }
     }
+
+    T* data() noexcept { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    std::size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+struct HostOptions {
+    explicit HostOptions(const std::size_t count)
+        : count(count),
+          numeric(count * kNumericFields,
+                  cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined),
+          types(count,
+                cudaHostAllocPortable | cudaHostAllocWriteCombined) {}
+
+    double* strike() noexcept { return numeric.data(); }
+    double* spot() noexcept { return numeric.data() + count; }
+    double* q() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 2 * count; }
+    double* r() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 3 * count; }
+    double* t() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 4 * count; }
+    double* vol() noexcept { return numeric.data() + 5 * count; }
+
+    const double* field(const std::size_t fieldIndex) const noexcept {
+        return numeric.data() + fieldIndex * count;
+    }
+
+    std::size_t count;
+    PinnedBuffer<double> numeric;
+    PinnedBuffer<int> types;
+};
+
+void generateOptions(HostOptions& options, const std::size_t globalBegin) {
+    // Each MPI rank first-touches its pinned input arrays in parallel.  The
+    // structure-of-arrays layout gives the CUDA kernel coalesced loads.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localIndex = 0;
+         localIndex < static_cast<std::int64_t>(options.count); ++localIndex) {
+        const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(localIndex);
+        const OptionInput option = makeOption(globalBegin + i);
+        options.types.data()[i] = option.type;
+        options.strike()[i] = option.strike;
+        options.spot()[i] = option.spot;
+        options.q()[i] = option.q;
+        options.r()[i] = option.r;
+        options.t()[i] = option.t;
+        options.vol()[i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
+}
+
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const int* __restrict__ types,
+                        const double* __restrict__ strike,
+                        const double* __restrict__ spot,
+                        const double* __restrict__ q,
+                        const double* __restrict__ r,
+                        const double* __restrict__ t,
+                        const double* __restrict__ vol,
+                        double* __restrict__ results,
+                        const std::size_t count) {
+    const std::size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<std::size_t>(blockDim.x) +
+                          threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double time = t[i];
+    const double sigma = vol[i];
+    if (time <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
+        results[i] = 0.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double stock = spot[i];
+    const double exercise = strike[i];
+    const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+    const double sigmaSqrtTime = sigma * sqrtTime;
+    const double d1 =
+        (log(stock / exercise) +
+         (r[i] - q[i] + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * time) /
+        sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double discountedStock = stock * exp(-q[i] * time);
+    const double discountedStrike = exercise * exp(-r[i] * time);
+    const double call =
+        discountedStock * cumulativeNormalDevice(d1) -
+        discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);
+
+    // Put-call parity avoids a divergent second formula while retaining the
+    // same Black-Scholes semantics for both option types.
+    results[i] = types[i] == CALL
+                     ? call
+                     : call - discountedStock + discountedStrike;
 }

+class GpuExecutor {
+public:
+    explicit GpuExecutor(const std::size_t optionCount)
+        : optionCount_(optionCount),
+          capacity_(std::min(optionCount, kChunkOptions)) {
+        if (optionCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const std::size_t chunkCount =
+            (optionCount_ + kChunkOptions - 1) / kChunkOptions;
+        streamCount_ = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::size_t>(kMaxStreams, chunkCount));
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams_[i],
+                                                  cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&numeric_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * kNumericFields * sizeof(double)));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&types_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * sizeof(int)));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&results_[i]),
+                                  capacity_ * sizeof(double)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    GpuExecutor(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
+    GpuExecutor& operator=(const GpuExecutor&) = delete;
+
+    ~GpuExecutor() {
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            cudaFree(numeric_[i]);
+            cudaFree(types_[i]);
+            cudaFree(results_[i]);
+            cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void execute(const HostOptions& options, double* hostResults) {
+        for (std::size_t chunkIndex = 0, begin = 0; begin < optionCount_;
+             ++chunkIndex, begin += kChunkOptions) {
+            const int streamIndex =
+                static_cast<int>(chunkIndex % streamCount_);
+            const cudaStream_t stream = streams_[streamIndex];
+            const std::size_t count =
+                std::min(kChunkOptions, optionCount_ - begin);
+
+            // A stream's fixed-size staging buffers can be reused once its
+            // previous D2H copy has completed. Other streams continue running.
+            if (chunkIndex >= static_cast<std::size_t>(streamCount_)) {
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+            }
+
+            for (std::size_t field = 0; field < kNumericFields; ++field) {
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(
+                    numeric_[streamIndex] + field * capacity_,
+                    options.field(field) + begin, count * sizeof(double),
+                    cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(types_[streamIndex],
+                                       options.types.data() + begin,
+                                       count * sizeof(int),
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+
+            const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                (count + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
+            blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+                types_[streamIndex], numeric_[streamIndex],
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 2 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 3 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 4 * capacity_,
+                numeric_[streamIndex] + 5 * capacity_, results_[streamIndex],
+                count);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostResults + begin,
+                                       results_[streamIndex],
+                                       count * sizeof(double),
+                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
+        }
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < streamCount_; ++i) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[i]));
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    std::size_t optionCount_ = 0;
+    std::size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    int streamCount_ = 0;
+    std::array<cudaStream_t, kMaxStreams> streams_{};
+    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> numeric_{};
+    std::array<int*, kMaxStreams> types_{};
+    std::array<double*, kMaxStreams> results_{};
+};
+
+double blackScholesHost(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
+    if (option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const double sqrtTime = std::sqrt(option.t);
+    const double sigmaSqrtTime = option.vol * sqrtTime;
+    const double d1 =
+        (std::log(option.spot / option.strike) +
+         (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t) /
+        sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtTime;
+    const auto cdf = [](const double x) {
+        return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
+    };
+    const double discountedStock = option.spot * std::exp(-option.q * option.t);
+    const double discountedStrike =
+        option.strike * std::exp(-option.r * option.t);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return discountedStock * cdf(d1) - discountedStrike * cdf(d2);
+    }
+    return discountedStrike * cdf(-d2) - discountedStock * cdf(-d1);
+}
+

         const double computed = results[i];

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
-    printf("Options:\n");

+void gatherResults(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
+                   const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                   const int rankCount, std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(total);
+    }
+
+    // MPI_Gatherv uses int counts and displacements. Windowing keeps both in
+    // range even when the global problem contains more than INT_MAX options.
+    constexpr std::size_t maxWindow = 1U << 30;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(rankCount));
+    }
+
+    for (std::size_t windowBegin = 0; windowBegin < total;
+         windowBegin += maxWindow) {
+        const std::size_t windowEnd =
+            std::min(total, windowBegin + maxWindow);
+        const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
+        const std::size_t sendBegin = std::max(windowBegin, localRange.begin);
+        const std::size_t sendEnd = std::min(windowEnd, localEnd);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendBegin
+                                  ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendBegin)
+                                  : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer =
+            sendCount == 0
+                ? localResults
+                : localResults + (sendBegin - localRange.begin);
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < rankCount; ++source) {
+                const Range sourceRange = rankRange(total, source, rankCount);
+                const std::size_t sourceEnd =
+                    sourceRange.begin + sourceRange.count;
+                const std::size_t receiveBegin =
+                    std::max(windowBegin, sourceRange.begin);
+                const std::size_t receiveEnd =
+                    std::min(windowEnd, sourceEnd);
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
+                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveEnd - receiveBegin)
+                        : 0;
+                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    receiveEnd > receiveBegin
+                        ? static_cast<int>(receiveBegin - windowBegin)
+                        : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(
+            sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+            rank == 0 ? globalResults.data() + windowBegin : nullptr,
+            rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+            rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+            MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    }
+}
+
+void gatherValidationPrefix(const double* localResults, const Range localRange,
+                            const std::size_t total, const int rank,
+                            std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxChecks = 10;
+    const std::size_t checkCount = std::min(maxChecks, total);
+    std::array<double, maxChecks> localValues{};
+    std::array<double, maxChecks> globalValues{};
+    const std::size_t localEnd = localRange.begin + localRange.count;
+    const std::size_t copyEnd = std::min(checkCount, localEnd);
+    for (std::size_t globalIndex = localRange.begin;
+         globalIndex < copyEnd; ++globalIndex) {
+        localValues[globalIndex] =
+            localResults[globalIndex - localRange.begin];
+    }
+
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(localValues.data(), globalValues.data(),
+                         static_cast<int>(checkCount), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.assign(globalValues.begin(),
+                                 globalValues.begin() + checkCount);
+    }
 }

+        } else if (validate) {
+            // Validation consumes only the same ten leading values as the
+            // original benchmark, avoiding a non-scalable all-results gather.
+            gatherValidationPrefix(localResults.data(), localRange, numOptions,
+                                   rank, globalResults);
+        }
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) {
+                print_results(globalResults, "OptionPrices");
+            }
+            if (validate) {
+                std::printf("Validating results...\n");
+                if (validateResults(numOptions, globalResults)) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                } else {
+                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                    exitCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
+    return exitCode;
 }

tokens used
95,098
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version while preserving the `black_scholes` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI partitions options evenly across ranks and maps node-local ranks to GPUs.
- OpenMP parallelizes host-side option generation.
- CUDA uses coalesced structure-of-arrays data, pinned memory, four asynchronous streams, chunked transfers, and a 256-thread pricing kernel.
- Rank 0 preserves output semantics; validation avoids gathering unnecessary global data.
- Large `MPI_Gatherv` results are windowed beyond `INT_MAX`.
- CMake now requires and links CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP with optimized native compilation.
